                get: function (resource_id) {
                    return tdLocalCache.data[resource_id];
                },
                set: function (resource_id, cachedData) {
                    tdLocalCache.remove(resource_id);
                    tdLocalCache.data[resource_id] = cachedData;
                }
            };
        })();

<script>
    ( function( $ ) {
        /* add smooth scrolling on anchor links ------------------------*/
        function scrollLink() {
          var nav = $('#on--page--nav');
          $('a.smooth--link, li.smooth--link > a').click(function() {
            if (location.pathname.replace(/^\//,'') === this.pathname.replace(/^\//,'') && location.hostname === this.hostname) {
              var target = $(this.hash);
              target = target.length ? target : $('[name=' + this.hash.slice(1) +']');
              if (target.length && nav.hasClass('sticky')) {
                $('html, body').animate({
                  scrollTop: target.offset().top - 150
                }, 700);
                return false;
              }
              else if (target.length) {
                $('html, body').animate({
                  scrollTop: target.offset().top - 150
                }, 700);
                return false;
              }
            }
          });
        }

        /* change height of magin on content-wrap dynamically --------------*/
        function dynamicMargin() {
            if ($(window).width() > 767) {
                //using the height of the logo container because menu height can be unreliable before font loads
                var header = $(".td-main-menu-logo");
                var margin = $(".td-main-content-wrap");
                margin.css("margin-top", header.outerHeight());
            }
        }

        $( document ).ready( scrollLink ); // .ready( dynamicMargin )
    })( jQuery );
    </script>
